# Prospects: Southern California

Generated 2026-10-07 by `_tools/prospect.py`. Filters: rating ≥ 4.5, reviews ≥ 150, chains skipped. 2474 restaurants checked, 565 leads.

Lead score = how bad the current site is × how good the restaurant is. Domain prices are Namecheap list prices from memory; confirm at checkout. Size and price are a rough guess from review count, price level and type: adjust after you meet them.

Leads marked ✓ were checked by hand (web search and opening their site) and are listed first. Pitch only those; the rest still need checking.

## Checked by hand: already have a good website (not leads)

- All About Pho - Fullerton — good website — https://www.allaboutpho.com/ — aboutpho.com redirects to allaboutpho.com, a BentoBox site with a Fullerton page (100 S Harbor Blvd Ste B, (909) 255-6626, daily 11am-9pm), menus, and online ordering through Toast and DoorDash.
- Acai Republic Fullerton — good website — https://acairepublic.com/store/acai-republic-fullerton — The Açaí Republic chain site lists this exact address (1109 N Harbor Blvd) with hours (daily 9am-10pm), a full menu, online pickup ordering, catering and an app.
- Boathouse on the Bay — good website — https://www.boathouseonthebay.com/ — Full, maintained site (Next.js on Vercel) with menus including sushi, hours, location, Toast online ordering, Toast reservations and waitlist, and events. It lists only 190 N Marina Dr, Long Beach, not the 4100 Donald Douglas Dr (Long Beach Airport) address in this listing.
- MOOD. — good website — https://mood-la.com/ — Probably has a site (unconfirmed): DNS shows mood-la.com and www.mood-la.com are a Toast Website (sites.toasttab.com), and this MOOD orders through Toast, but a bot check blocked the page, so check it in a browser before deciding (moodla.com and mood.la are for sale or parked).
- Camp Coffee Company — good website — https://www.campcoffeecompany.com/ — The Google-listed Square address redirects to their own domain, a Square Online site with drink and food menu pages, an Order Now shop, merchandise, hours, and the 101 N Cleveland St address and phone.
- Joe Aguilar’s Templo Del Sol — good website — https://joeaguilarstemplodelsol.com/ — A well-built, current bilingual site (copyright 2026) with the address, phone, hours, a 33-dish menu (no prices) and DoorDash/Uber Eats links for delivery.
- Café Vahik — good website — https://cafevahik.com/ — A current site (© 2026, built with a website builder) with a full priced menu, hours and the 6780 La Jolla Blvd address, but the www.cafevahik.com link on Google has no DNS record and the site's phone link is a placeholder (+1 555-123-4567).
- Golden Egg Cafe — good website — https://thegoldeneggcafe.com/ — Possible site, not confirmed: thegoldeneggcafe.com is a live Toast Website (www points to sites.toasttab.com), but a bot check blocked the page, so it may be a different Golden Egg Cafe; check in a browser that it is the 2009 E Main St Ventura location before treating it as theirs or pitching.
